# Yuddhakāṇḍa: Leonov docx vs valmikiramayan.net — comparison report

_Created: 12-07-2026_

> Sources: `RussianRamayana/data/Ramayana_shestaya_kniga_sanskritskiy_text.docx` (Leonov's edited
> Southern-recension text, IAST) vs <https://www.valmikiramayan.net/utf8/yuddha/> (K.M.K. Murthy's
> Southern-recension text, Devanāgarī), all 128 sargas. Verse identity/order does NOT match
> 1:1 between the two sources (Leonov's per-sarga numbering diverges from the site's own),
> so comparison used **content alignment** (difflib LCS + fuzzy token-Jaccard rescue on the
> unmatched remainder — same method as `CommentaryStrategies/scripts/compare_editions.py`),
> canonicalized via the org `sanskrit_util.nfold` (`CommentaryStrategies/scripts/sa_align.py`),
> not naive same-locus lookup (which produced ~95% spurious "differences" on a first pass).

## Summary

| | Count |
|---|---:|
| Leonov docx verses (parsed) | 5728 |
| valmikiramayan.net verses (parsed) | 5359 |
| Identical (canon-exact, content-aligned) | 167 |
| Variant pairs (aligned, textually different) | 3808 |
|  — near-identical (sim ≥ 0.9, likely spelling/sandhi) | 3125 |
|  — minor edit (sim 0.6–0.9, likely genuine reading variant) | 484 |
|  — major difference (sim < 0.6, substantial or misaligned) | 199 |
| Leonov-only (no site counterpart found) | 1753 |
| Site-only (no Leonov counterpart found) | 1254 (+130 sarga-end colophons, not real verses) |

**Caveats:** (1) `leonov_only`/`site-only` includes some residual verse-boundary/off-by-one
segmentation noise from automated pāda-grouping on both sides, not only genuine additions —
spot-checking is advised before treating any single entry as a confirmed omission. (2) The
"site-only" colophon lines (`iti vālmīki rāmāyaṇe ... sargaḥ`) are expected — Leonov's text
does not transcribe per-sarga colophons.

## Major differences (similarity < 0.6) — most likely to be real substantive divergences

199 pairs. Low similarity can mean either a genuine substantial textual difference
or a local verse-alignment mismatch (adjacent verse content) — check locus context before citing.
